# Skills · L · part 96

715 entries, long-read-sequencing-preprocessing to longbridge-ownership. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **long-read-sequencing-preprocessing**: Use when preprocessing nanopore (PromethION) FASTQ reads from enzymatic methyl-seq (EMseq) data for alignment, where unmethylated cytosines were converted to thymine and a C-to-T / G-to-A dual-strand reference conversion is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sequencing-preprocessing))
- **long-read-sequencing-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking third-generation SV/deletion callers needs ground-truth data at multiple coverage depths (e.g. 90x/65x/45x/20x) and specified variant length ranges, so recall/precision/F1 can be computed against a known truth set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sequencing-read-simulation))
- **long-read-sequencing-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a somatic SV caller against alternatives and no matched tumor/control long-read data with a validated truth set exists; simulation lets you control tumor purity (e.g. 0–100%) and sequence yield (e.g. 10–50x). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sequencing-simulation))
- **long-read-short-read-barcode-linkage**: Use when a hybrid scRNA-seq experiment splits one barcoded library into Illumina SR and ONT/PacBio LR pools, and LRs must be assigned to cellular barcodes shared with SRs; especially when existing tools (FLAMES, SiCeLoRe) are too slow for many reads/cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-short-read-barcode-linkage))
- **long-read-short-read-benchmark-comparison**: Use when you need to quantify how a fast barcode-matching method (scTagger, e=2) deviates from an exact DP baseline and from FLAMES on hybrid LR-SR scRNA-seq data, e.g. reproducing Figure 6 / Table S1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-short-read-benchmark-comparison))
- **long-read-simulation-error-models**: Use when you need ground-truth-controlled synthetic PacBio CCS/CLR or Oxford Nanopore reads, optionally with embedded SVs (deletions/insertions/inversions), to benchmark mapper location accuracy or SV-calling performance across coverages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-simulation-error-models))
- **long-read-simulation-pacbio-clr**: Use when benchmarking long-read structural-variant mappers against a known ground truth: simulate inversion SVs in a reference genome and generate coverage-controlled PacBio CLR reads spanning the breakpoints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-simulation-pacbio-clr))
- **long-read-simulation-parameter-grid-design**: Use when you need to isolate the effect of long-read data characteristics (read length, coverage, accuracy) on a pipeline's performance by simulating reads against a reference genome with controlled parameter values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-simulation-parameter-grid-design))
- **long-read-simulation-parameterization**: Use when building a truth-set benchmark of non-reference TE insertions for long-read callers, i.e. when you need simulated PacBio/ONT BAM files with known insertion positions, types, and coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-simulation-parameterization))
- **long-read-single-cell-isoform-quantification**: Use when starting from long-read (10x ONT or PacBio) single-cell FASTQs and you need cell barcode demultiplexing, isoform reconstruction/quantification, and per-cell or pseudobulk isoform count matrices without matching short-read data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-single-cell-isoform-quantification))
- **long-read-single-cell-preprocessing**: Use when input is 10x single-cell long-read FASTQ data (Oxford Nanopore or PacBio) and the goal is cell barcode demultiplexing, alignment, isoform/gene quantification, and emptydrops cell filtering without matched short-read data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-single-cell-preprocessing))
- **long-read-single-cell-variant-calling**: Use when single-cell cDNA library FASTQs (10x Genomics whole-transcriptome or targeted amplicon long reads) must be genotyped for somatic SNVs per cell barcode, e.g., tracking AML subclones or comparing genotyping rates to Illumina-based GoT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-single-cell-variant-calling))
- **long-read-snv-calling**: Use when PacBio HiFi/CCS reads (e.g. hifi BAM or ccsmeth modBAM) must be converted to a phased variant layer so reads can be haplotagged for allele-specific methylation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-snv-calling))
- **long-read-snv-variant-calling**: Use when input is a sorted, indexed BAM/CRAM of long error-prone reads (PacBio or ONT) aligned to an indexed reference FASTA and you need phased, diploid SNV calls with precision/recall evaluation against GIAB truth sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-snv-variant-calling))
- **long-read-softclip-barcode-motif-search**: Use when assigning 10x Genomics cell barcodes and UMIs to long ONT/PacBio reads from 5'-anchored whole-transcriptome cDNA libraries whose cDNA alignments retain 5' softclips, as in nanoranger genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-softclip-barcode-motif-search))
- **long-read-somatic-sv-detection**: Use when paired tumor/matched-control long-read sequencing data (ONT PromethION FASTQ/BAM or PacBio) must be mined for somatic SVs at single-base resolution, especially single breakend calls such as HBV/viral integrations, LINE1-mediated rearrangements, or centromeric events. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-somatic-sv-detection))
- **long-read-splice-aware-alignment**: Use when FLNC reads (post CCS/LIMA/refine and TAMA polyA cleanup, in FASTA form) must be spliced-aligned to a genome and a reference GTF may or may not exist, requiring a dispatch between uLTRA and minimap2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-splice-aware-alignment))
- **long-read-splice-aware-genome-alignment**: Use when PacBio full-length transcript reads (isoseq_flnc.Transcript.fastq) must be spliced-aligned to a reference genome before isoform annotation or single-cell quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-splice-aware-genome-alignment))
- **long-read-splice-junction-extraction**: Use when evaluating how many splice junctions predicted from long-read Iso-seq transcript alignments match a reference annotation (e.g., Ensembl hg38 GTF), especially comparing aligners like TAGET, minimap2, and GMAP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-splice-junction-extraction))
- **long-read-splice-site-merging**: Reference for defining consensus exon coordinates (splice start/end positions) from long-read BAM/GFF3/BED6 alignments when splice sites are unknown a priori, e.g. SIRV or minigene splicing assays analyzed one gene at a time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-splice-site-merging))
- **long-read-spliced-read-alignment**: Use when starting from cellbarcoded long-read FASTQ (10x ONT, or PacBio with appropriate options) and a scywalker/genomecomb reference directory, needing spliced genome alignments before isoform reconstruction and per-cell counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-spliced-read-alignment))
- **long-read-spliced-read-alignment**: Use when long-read transcript sequencing reads (FASTQ, e.g. PacBio HiFi or ONT) must be spliced-aligned to a reference genome (FASTA) before isoform detection or benchmarking of tools such as VIsoQLR, FLAIR or StringTie2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-spliced-read-alignment))
- **long-read-splicing**: Analyzes alternative splicing from PacBio Iso-Seq (HiFi, Kinnex/MAS-Iso-seq) and Oxford Nanopore (direct cDNA, direct RNA, R10.4.1+) long-read RNA-seq with full-isoform resolution. Tools include FLAIR (correct/collapse/quantify/diffSplice for PacBio + ONT), IsoQuant (de-novo or annotation-guided isoform discovery 2024 SOTA), Bambu (annotation-aware Bayesian discovery + quantification with Novel Discovery Rate), SQANTI3/SQANTI-LR (isoform classification: FSM/ISM/NIC/NNC + artifact flags), rMATS-long (event calling on long-read isoforms), and minimap2 (-ax splice:hq for HiFi; -ax splice -k14 fo… ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/alternative-splicing-long-read-splicing) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **long-read-splicing-order-quantification**: Use when quantifying splicing order (AISO) around user-specified exons from long-read (e.g. ONT direct-RNA) alignments, and when long-read upfi/dofi or Fupfi distributions must be harmonized with short-read reference patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-splicing-order-quantification))
- **long-read-split-read-extraction**: Use when you have a PacBio/ONT long-read bam or cram aligned to a reference and need to recover split-read signatures and insertion sequences for candidate non-reference TE (or any insertion) calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-split-read-extraction))
- **long-read-split-vs-gapped-alignment-interpretation**: Use when long-read SAM/BAM alignments contain gapped CIGAR strings (I/D operations &gt;10 bp) at a locus of interest and these within-read indels must be extracted, coordinated, and annotated rather than relying on split-read (N/soft-clip) breakpoints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-split-vs-gapped-alignment-interpretation))
- **long-read-structural-variant-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare long-read nanopore TE/SV detection tools against an established truth set — e.g. replicating Table 1 of the RetroInspector paper where PALMER (F1 60.72%) and GraffiTE (F1 48.40%) are benchmarked against RetroInspector's cuteSV+Sniffles2 callset (F1 83. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-structural-variant-benchmarking))
- **long-read-structural-variant-calling**: Use when calling and genotyping germline structural variants from mapped PacBio HiFi reads (e.g. HG002 Revio data), or when benchmarking SV caller accuracy (F1, precision, recall) against GIAB T2T or CMRG benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-structural-variant-calling--dd2322b36136500f))
- **long-read-structural-variant-calling**: Use when calling deletions and insertions from ONT long-read FASTQ samples against a reference genome (e.g. hg38), when a consensus meta-caller should outperform single SV callers, or when benchmarking SV callsets with Truvari against ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-structural-variant-calling--5decf0a3f81388b2))
- **long-read-structural-variant-workflow**: Use when long-read FASTQ data must be aligned, called by multiple structural-variant methods, verified per caller for read-depth support of copy-number events, merged, and compared with an external truth or high-confidence call set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/workflows/long-read-structural-variant))
- **long-read-subread-deconcatenation**: Use when input FASTQ/BAM contains multimer or concatenated long reads (e.g., PacBio MAS-seq 15-mer arrays or ONT multimers) and multiple transcript-level records must be recovered from a single read. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-subread-deconcatenation))
- **long-read-sv-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or reproducing SV caller performance on long-read (PacBio HiFi/CLR, ONT) datasets where a truth or high-confidence callset exists and recall/precision/F1/GT_F1 comparisons are the deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-benchmarking))
- **long-read-sv-callset-qc**: Use when a VolcanoSV VCF from simulated long-read data (VISOR/PBSIM hg19) must be QC'd for INS-vs-DUP mislabeling, or when explaining/benchmarking elevated DUP F1 and GT_F1 against SVIM-asm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-callset-qc))
- **long-read-sv-callset-quality-verification**: Use when a Truvari-based SV caller ranking (e.g. sawfish vs Sniffles2 vs pbsv on HG002 32-fold HiFi WGS) needs confirmation with an independent benchmarking engine before trusting the conclusion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-callset-quality-verification))
- **long-read-sv-consensus-evaluation**: Use when a collapsed consensus SV VCF (e.g. from ConsensuSV-ONT's vcfs output folder) must be labelled TP/FP against an HGSVC benchmark VCF to train or evaluate the CNN variant filter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-consensus-evaluation))
- **long-read-sv-joint-genotyping**: Use when multiple mapped HiFi (CCS) sample BAMs are available and the goal is jointly genotyped SV calls whose inheritance can be checked against a known pedigree, or when comparing SV caller concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-joint-genotyping))
- **long-read-sv-validation**: Use when SV calls (e.g. deletions from Supernova2 assemblies or SvABA/Long Ranger callsets) must be independently validated against long-read sequencing data before benchmarking or release. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-validation))
- **long-read-tandem-repeat-profiling**: Use when haplotype-resolved BAM files from ONT or PacBio whole-genome sequencing must be scanned de novo for tandem repeats with motif length &gt;6 bp (mini-satellites) without prior motif or locus knowledge. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-tandem-repeat-profiling))
- **long-read-taxonomic-alignment**: Use when quality-controlled ARG-containing long reads (Nanopore or PacBio) need candidate species assignments and alignment scores against a GTDB-derived reference, as the host-collection step of an ARG profiling pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-taxonomic-alignment))
- **long-read-tcr-sequence-detection**: Use when you have ONT (or PacBio) FASTQs from targeted amplification of a 10x Genomics V(DJ) cDNA library and need per-cell-barcode TCR calls, e.g. tumor-infiltrating T cell TCR profiling with nanoranger. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-tcr-sequence-detection))
- **long-read-telomere-sequencing-table-parsing**: Use when you have demultiplexed telomap TSV output from WGS or telobait-captured long-read data and need per-patient mean telomere length metrics for group-level statistics such as age correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-telomere-sequencing-table-parsing))
- **long-read-to-contig-alignment**: Use when corrected long reads must be mapped to haplotype-collapsed contigs to obtain pileup-based heterozygous SNP sites for read grouping and inconsistent-overlap detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-to-contig-alignment))
- **long-read-to-contig-mapping-statistics**: Use when a labelled ntLink PAF mapping file (read-to-contig mappings generated with paf=True) must be summarized to validate long-read mappings against expected statistics from the ntLink article's Table 5. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-to-contig-mapping-statistics))
- **long-read-to-reference-alignment**: Use when full-length (~1500 bp) 16S PacBio CCS reads must be mapped to a single 16S reference to quantify per-position substitutions that distinguish intragenomic 16S gene copies within one genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/long-read-to-reference-alignment))
- **long-read-transcript-alignment**: Use when input is PacBio Iso-seq full-length transcript FASTA (CCS reads) that must be aligned to a reference genome (e.g., hg38.fa) with splice-aware annotation of transcripts, junctions, and read classifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcript-alignment))
- **long-read-transcript-alignment-filtering**: Use when raw PacBio/ONT FASTQ reads with single-cell barcodes must be splice-aligned to a reference genome and filtered to high-confidence full-length spliced isoforms before per-cell-type isoform quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-transcript-alignment-filtering))
- **long-read-transcript-catalog-analysis**: Use when you have a filtered long-read isoform database (e.g., IsoPops DB with per-isoform read counts) and need to quantify how each gene's expression is distributed across its isoforms or rank genes by isoform diversity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcript-catalog-analysis))
- **long-read-transcript-error-correction**: Use when long noisy reads from targeted PacBio Iso-Seq (or ONT/R2C2 CCS-style) sequencing of highly similar gene copies or amplicons must be error-corrected and collapsed into finished consensus transcripts, including isoform-capture versus sequencing-depth analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcript-error-correction))
- **long-read-transcript-isoform-quantification**: Use when you need isoform-level expression estimates from ONT direct-RNA (RNA004) reads aligned to a transcriptome, or when validating a nanopore simulator by comparing ESPRESSO-estimated isoform abundances to ground-truth transcript abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcript-isoform-quantification))
- **long-read-transcriptome-annotation**: Use when you have a merged StringTie GTF assembly from hybrid long-read (PacBio) plus Illumina RNA-seq and need coding/noncoding classification, GTF reconciliation against a reference annotation, and identification of novel genes absent from that reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcriptome-annotation))
- **long-read-transcriptome-isoform-detection**: Use when analyzing long-read RNA-seq (nanopore or PacBio Iso-Seq) BAM/GFF3/BED6 alignments of selected genes or spike-ins (e.g., SIRVs) to identify and quantify known or novel isoforms and tune detection thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-transcriptome-isoform-detection))
- **long-read-transcriptome-reconstruction**: Use when you have BAM files of aligned full-length LRTS transcripts (e.g. PacBio SequelII hepatocyte CTL/VPA data) and a gff3/gtf reference annotation, and need gene/transcript reconstruction with novel-transcript classification and tunable filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcriptome-reconstruction))
- **long-read-transcriptome-sequencing-qc**: Use when raw LRTS bam files (aligned full-length transcripts, e.g. PacBio SequelII IsoSeq data) need QC assessment, filter tagging, or when a saturation/depth analysis is needed to decide if rare transcripts (e.g. 1 TPM) are reliably observed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcriptome-sequencing-qc))
- **long-read-transposable-element-calling**: Use when you have a long-read BAM/CRAM aligned to a reference genome and need to detect non-reference TE insertions, or when benchmarking sTELLeR against TLDR/xTEA on PacBio data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transposable-element-calling))
- **long-read-variant-phasing-haplotagging**: Use when calling SNVs from ONT long-read BAM alignments where low-quality candidate loci must be resolved via haplotype-aware consensus sequences, or when phased, haplotagged alignments are needed to build per-haplotype local assemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-variant-phasing-haplotagging))
- **long-read-vs-short-read-isoform-benchmarking**: Use when a long-read study reports isoforms for a complex, multi-isoform gene locus and you need to test whether per-cell short-read Illumina assembly recovers those same isoforms or produces contigs absent from the long-read set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-vs-short-read-isoform-benchmarking))
- **long-read-vs-short-read-splicing-comparison**: Use when you have a reconstructed long-read transcriptome (e.g. IsoTools pickle from PacBio LRTS) plus paired short-read RNA-seq, and want to know whether PSI values agree per splicing event category. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-vs-short-read-splicing-comparison))
- **long-read-wgs-pipeline-execution**: Use when raw long-read whole-genome reads (PacBio HiFi or targeted ONT FASTQ/BAM) from human rare-disease samples must be turned into aligned BAMs, phased variant calls, de novo assemblies and methylation profiles via a containerized Nextflow run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-wgs-pipeline-execution))
- **long-reads**: Process and analyze long-read sequencing data from PacBio and Oxford Nanopore platforms. Use when working with long-read sequencing data (&gt;10kb reads), performing de novo assembly, or detecting structural variants. ([honeyvig/skillsbench](https://github.com/honeyvig/skillsbench/tree/HEAD/shared_workspace/dna-variant-calling/environment/skills/long-reads))
- **long-reconfig-database**: Debug Azure SQL Managed Instance long reconfiguration database issues (TSGCL0212) where a database partition reconfiguration takes longer than expected. Applies ONLY to Managed Instance incidents (AppTypeName Worker.CL*). - **longbridge-hkipo-analysis**: HK IPO 打新分析 — 港股新股是否值得申购的"四维评估"诊断（定价合理性 / 发行质量 / 市场时机 / 基本面展望）。输出推荐 / 中性 / 回避三档评级 + 招股书精华 + 风险提示 + 申购参考，散户友好、结论前置。覆盖三类场景：单只新股深度评估、近期打新日历浏览、未中签后的次新股追涨判断。数据优先用 Longbridge CLI（ipo detail / ipo subscriptions / ipo wait-listing / ipo listed / peer-comparison / news / quote / kline / index-quote 等），CLI 缺失项用 MCP fallback；仍然不可得的（招股书 TAM、基石公告原文、回拨倍数、暗盘价、承销商行业排名）用 WebSearch 兜底。**报告结尾固定输出「数据来源明细」附录**，每个数字可溯源到行号 + 抓取时间 + 期间。仅覆盖港股主板与 GEM，不涉及美股 / A 股 IPO；涉及孖展（融资打新）时必须主动提示杠杆风险。Triggers: "打新", "港股打新", "新股申购", "新股申購", "新股", "打新分析", "新股分析", "招股", "招股书", "招股書", "基石投资者", "基石投資者", "国际配售", "國際配售", "公开发售", "公開發售",… ([shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory](https://github.com/shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/longbridge-hkipo-analysis))
